# Skills · M · part 115

805 entries, mvp-9-phases to my-first-skill. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **mvp-9-phases**: Nine-phase procedure for creating any product, tool, service or project from scratch through interrogation. The agent never decides what enters the MVP — it asks, proposes, measures, documents; the user decides. Applies to any language, platform or domain. Invoke when the user asks to create a new project/product/tool, or when about to write the first file of an empty repository. Do NOT invoke for modifications to existing projects. ([OnCeUponTry/CC-STARTUP-SKILL](https://github.com/OnCeUponTry/CC-STARTUP-SKILL/tree/HEAD/plugins/cc-startup/skills/mvp-9-phases))
